# UI conformance register

Phase C of [`spec/ui-conformance-plan.md`](../../spec/ui-conformance-plan.md). Every finding is
one row; every row is assigned to exactly one work package. This is the only place findings live.
Builders close rows; validators add rows; the lead confirms or rejects.

**Evidence.** Emulator screenshots taken 2026-09-07 on a Pixel 6 / API 34 AVD (`ort_audit`,
port 5554) of the app at `6aaa608`, and the source under `app/src/main/kotlin/org/ort/app/` at
`bff688b` (which includes audit fixes F-002, F-004, F-017, F-018, F-019 merged during phase A).
Each row names the artboard it was compared against; the artboards are under
[`design/canvas/`](../../design/canvas/) and the tokens in [`design/design-guide.md`](../../design/design-guide.md).

**Severity.** `halt` — wrong or misleading to the operator (constitution I) · `spec` — an
FR / P / F not met · `design` — the artboard not matched · `polish`.

**Status.** `open` · `building` (a builder owns it) · `fixed` (builder reports it closed, lead
not yet confirmed) · `closed` (confirmed on the emulator) · `rejected` (lead disagrees; reason
in the row).

---

## Theme, manifest, scaffold — WP1

| id | screen | artboard | what is wrong | sev | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R-001 | every | any phone board | No `android:theme` in the manifest, so every Activity renders a platform `DeviceDefault` ActionBar titled "Offline Radio Transcriber" *above* the Compose top bar. Every screen shows two headers. Screenshot `03-now.png`. | halt | closed |
| R-002 | launch | `Setup-Mic` | `MainActivity` is an unthemed `TextView` reading "scaffold running… Requesting microphone access…" on the platform ground, then fires bare OS prompts. Screenshot `01-launch.png`. | design | closed |
| R-003 | every drawer destination | `Main`, `Log` headers | The Compose `TopAppBar` carries the destination label as a title, and the screen body repeats it 27px below. "Now" / "Log" / "Search" each appear twice. The artboard header is icons only — drawer, live dot + elapsed, search — with no title text. Sweep @669628a slice 1: confirmed absent in tour run 6. | design | closed |
| R-004 | every | `Icons` | The drawer control is the text glyph `=` in body type. The artboard is the 21px three-line stroke icon at `text/icon` in a 44px target. Same for search: absent. Sweep @669628a slice 1: confirmed absent in tour run 6. | design | closed |
| R-005 | every | `Type` | `OrtType.typography` maps M3 `bodyMedium` to the 12.5px caption and `bodyLarge` to 15px, so every screen that asks for `MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium` gets caption size for body copy. The ramp has no 14 / 14.5 / 11.5 / 17px roles, which the boards use for controls, row titles, sub-lines and figures. Platform fallback faces are correct per constitution V; weights and tracking must still match §4 of the guide. Sweep @669628a slice 1: confirmed absent in tour run 6. | design | closed |
| R-006 | every | `Tokens` | `OrtTheme` sets a colour scheme but no root `Surface`; the window ground is whatever `DeviceDefault` gives. Screens must sit on `bg/screen` `oklch(0.155 0.008 250)` (≈ `#202329`) from the theme, not from the OS. | design | closed |
| R-007 | every | — | `ReaderActivity` is `exported="false"` and `MainActivity` `finish()`es after launching it; the back stack after a cold start is `Reader` alone, which is right — but a process death mid-session relaunches into `MainActivity`'s permission flow rather than the reader. Verify and route. Sweep @669628a: cannot judge from a capture — process-death back-stack routing is lifecycle behaviour; covered by the real-Activity smoke tests instead. Sweep @669628a: lifecycle behaviour, not visual; covered by the real-Activity smoke tests. | polish | closed |
| R-008 | every | guide §12 | Found on the emulator after WP1 merged (`8ad7cc1`): status-bar clock and icons render dark on the dark ground on both activities — `enableEdgeToEdge()` with no arguments chooses icon colour from the OS night-mode setting, and this app is dark-only. Both activities must pass `SystemBarStyle.dark(TRANSPARENT)` for status and navigation bars, and `themes.xml` must set `windowLightStatusBar`/`windowLightNavigationBar` false. Screenshot `wp1-02-now.png`. | design | closed |

WP7 (diagnose) → `:data`. DIAGNOSED (WP7): the API 34 emulator's platform SQLite has no fts5 module ("no such module: fts5" at CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE); `OrtDatabase` assumes fts5 on minSdk 26 and only builds the index in onCreate. Lead decision: bundle a SQLite with FTS5 in `:data` (also fixes Robolectric's missing fts5). :data bc9bc51/c009d7e: bundled `androidx.sqlite:sqlite-bundled` with FTS5 behind `BundledSQLiteDriver` (Room callbacks now run via `inWriteTransaction`/`execRaw`), 10 s busy timeout, schema v4. Text search works on API 34 and under Robolectric. V5 search half confirms on device. V5 search pass 4 @fc693af: confirmed — text and callsign queries return grouped, highlighted results with honest counts (`search-corpus/Q03-results-clean-pass4.png`, `Q03-callsign-pass4.png`). | halt | closed |

| R-612 | S03 Setup-Notify scrolled at 2.0 | `Setup-Notify`, guide §5 | Review 6 @742a630 (lead-verified, re-described from the reviewer's report): the scrolled body text passes UNDER the pinned title block, which has no opaque background — the line "does, and that notification is" is half-occluded by the subtitle's bottom edge and the sentence before it is hidden entirely. Not glyph distortion and not R-340's ghosting: a scroll container that extends beneath a header drawn without a background. Give the pinned header an opaque `bg/base` (or clip the scroll container to start below it) on every Setup step. `setup-verified/S03-notify@2x-end.png`. WP9. WP9 afcf6f3 DISPROVED the lead's diagnosis with evidence: a magenta-background diagnostic shows the body paragraph's layout box starts cleanly below the header — there is no overlap, and two fixes built on that premise produced byte-identical screenshots on device. The distorted line sits inside its own correctly-placed box as the topmost scroll-revealed line, i.e. a glyph rasterisation artefact. Reclassified into the R-220/R-280/R-340 emulator-software-renderer family — all four need the same check on reference hardware before any of them is treated as a product defect. WP9 kept an explicit opaque header background (harmless) and a structural R_612 regression guard. | design | open |
| R-613 | N04 Capture-Status @2x-end under `storage-warn` | `Capture-Status`, `Fail-Storage`, FR-UI-7, cf. R-300/R-550 | Review 6 @742a630: R-550 regressed under the board-width density (R-600) — at 2.0 with the storage banner the Tier sub-line is clipped by the fixed "Low storage" toast and the Storage and Battery rows FR-UI-7 requires are never reached, where V-final measured 21 dp of clearance before. The toast's height must be published to the scroll container as bottom padding (WP11b's own R-550 note) rather than relying on the weight split, which now leaves nothing spare. `storage-warn/N04-capture-status@2x-end.png`. WP11b + WP4. WP11b @ba884d8 corrected the premise with proof, no code shipped: the "Low storage" element is `LiveBar` (WP2 component, WP4 data) rendered as a PLAIN SIBLING in `CaptureStatusScreen.kt`'s Column, not a FailureHost overlay — so there is nothing in FailureHost to publish, and outer padding on `CaptureStatusContent` shrinks the weighted scroll region 1:1 instead of adding clearance (measured -12 dp overflow when tried in f0c9ece). The fix is one line INSIDE the scrollable Column's own trailing padding in `CaptureStatusScreen.kt`, the shape `FailureActionBarScaffold.kt`'s kdoc already documents for R-292. Routed to WP4. The banner must size to its content (bounded by a fraction of the screen and scrollable inside itself beyond that) and the content beneath must be offset by the banner's real height, never overlapped. `validation/V11/rig-bt-lost/F09-rig-bt-lost-now*.png`, `bt-audio-dropped/F23-now*.png`. Root cause: the 40% height cap (`BANNER_MAX_HEIGHT_FRACTION`) was too small for a title + two-sentence body + retry clause + two actions at font scale 2.0, so the *only* way to reach either action was scrolling the banner's own small internal region — but a real swipe anywhere in the destination's own much larger scrollable content beneath scrolls that instead, leaving the actions unreachable in practice. Raised the cap to 55% (comfortably fits F9's whole real message and both actions at 2.0 on the reference device without any scroll at all); for anything still taller, the scroll hint (18dp, amber-tinted on a filled pill, up from 14dp/`textSecondary` with no background) is now large and high-contrast enough that reviewer E1's own "not visible in either frame" complaint cannot recur — confirmed directly on device (`emulator-5558`, `rig-bt-lost`, hash-verified install; `emulator-5556` was contended by a concurrent session at capture time, see this round's report). New 2.0 NATIVE test at `w390dp-h844dp-420dpi` (the tour AVD's own width) seeds F9's real `attempt`/`ofTotal`/`nextRetryInMillis` and asserts the hint node exists and sits inside the banner's own bounds, then scrolls to it and asserts both actions are displayed. F23 half remains closed (run 5) — untouched by this round's changes since it shares the same mechanism at a shorter message that already fit. Was: fixed - WPF `77013ab8`: `BannerOverlay`'s own cap and offset math were already structurally correct (R-178's own `>=` case held) — on device, an `onGloballyPositioned` probe on both the banner and the destination content found the real `ScreenHeader` height and the banner's own internal `HEADER_HEIGHT + failureScreenInset()` clearance do not exactly cancel, leaving just enough slack for a maxed-out banner's last (cut-off) line to sit flush against the heading below with zero visible gap, reading as an overlap though nothing painted past the banner's own bottom. `onHeightMeasured` now reports its measured height plus `BANNER_CONTENT_CLEARANCE` (`OrtSpacing.xl`, 30dp — comfortably clears the ~20dp slack measured on the reference device); a down-chevron scroll hint (`failure-banner-scroll-hint`) shows whenever the banner's own scroll state can still scroll forward and clears once scrolled to the end, addressing "no scroll affordance reaches it" (the banner was already reachable by a real swipe — confirmed directly — nothing on screen said so). 2.0 NATIVE tests assert a real minimum gap (not just `>=`) between the banner and the content below, and that the hint shows/hides correctly; confirmed fixed on `emulator-5556` (`rig-bt-lost`, hash-verified install) — screenshot in this round's report. F23 shares the same `BannerOverlay`/`Banner` mechanism, not verified separately on device this round.. Run 5 (C3): F23 half closed — real gap above "Tonight" and the scroll-hint chevron at the cut; F9 half has no 2.0 capture (R-960 adds it).
